String((item as { msg: unknown }).msg)\n          : String(item)\n      )\n      .join(\", \");\n  }\n  return fallback;\n}\n\nexport default function RegisterForm() {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const { login } = useAuth();\n\n  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(\"\");\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState(\"\");\n  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(\"\");\n  const [password, setPassword] = useState(\"\");\n  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n\n  async function handleRegister(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n\n    const trimmedName = fullName.trim();\n    const trimmedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();\n    const trimmedPhone = phone.trim();\n\n    if (trimmedName.length < 2) {\n      setError(\"Please enter your full name.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (!/^[0-9]{10}$/.test(trimmedPhone)) {\n      setError(\"Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (password.length < 6) {\n      setError(\"Password must be at least 6 characters.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (password !== confirmPassword) {\n      setError(\"Passwords do not match.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setLoading(true);\n\n    try {\n      const registerRes = await publicFetch(\"/auth/register\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          full_name: trimmedName,\n          email: trimmedEmail,\n          phone: trimmedPhone,\n          password,\n        }),\n      });\n\n      const registerData = await registerRes.json().catch(() => null);\n\n      if (!registerRes.ok) {\n        setError(\n          extractError(\n            registerData?.detail,\n            \"Registration failed. Please try again.\"\n          )\n        );\n        setLoading(false);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Existing Phase 2 flow: register → login → AuthContext JWT\n      const loginRes = await publicFetch(\"/auth/login\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          email: trimmedEmail,\n          password,\n        }),\n      });\n\n      const loginData = await loginRes.json().catch(() => null);\n\n      if (!loginRes.ok || !loginData?.access_token) {\n        setError(\n          \"Account created. Please log in with your credentials.\"\n        );\n        setLoading(false);\n        router.push(\"/login\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      login(\n        {\n          name: trimmedName,\n          email: trimmedEmail,\n          phone: trimmedPhone,\n        },\n        loginData.access_token\n      );\n\n      router.push(\"/\");\n    } catch {\n      setError(\"Unable to connect to Authentication Implementation\n\n### What exists\n- **Password hashing:** bcrypt via passlib (`E:/campusbite/campusbite/backend/app/auth/security.py`)\n- **JWT:** HS256, 24-hour expiry via `python-jose`\n- **Login flows:** customers (`/auth`), restaurant owners (`/restaurant-owner`), delivery partners (`/delivery`)\n\n### JWT payload differences\n| Actor | `sub` claim | Other claims |\n|-------|-------------|--------------|\n| Customer | MongoDB `_id` | `email`, `full_name` |\n| Restaurant owner | `email` | (none) |\n| Delivery partner | MongoDB `_id` | `email`, `name`, `role: \"delivery_partner\"` |\n\n### What is missing\n- **No auth middleware** — `decode_access_token()` exists but is **never used**\n- **No `Depends()` / `HTTPBearer`** on any route\n- **No role-based access control** — `role` is set in delivery JWT but never enforced\n- `E:/campusbite/campusbite/backend/app/auth/auth.py` and `auth/__init__.py` are **empty**\n- Tokens are issued on login but **not required** for any API call\n\n### Middleware\nOnly **CORS** (`allow_origins: [\"http://localhost:3000\"]`). No auth middleware.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Order System — Implementation Status\n\n### Works (fully wired to MongoDB)\n- Place order (`POST /orders/`) — sets `Pending`, generates 4-digit OTP, timestamps\n- List/filter: all orders, by customer phone, by restaurant email, single order\n- Status machine with enforced transitions in `update_order_status()`:\n  - `Pending` → `Accepted` / `Cancelled`\n  - `Accepted` → `Preparing` / `Cancelled`\n  - `Preparing` → `Ready for Pickup` / `Cancelled`\n  - `Assigned` → `Picked Up`\n  - `Picked Up` → `Out for Delivery`\n  - `Out for Delivery` → `Delivered` (via OTP only)\n- Restaurant **cannot** set `Delivered` directly (blocked in model)\n- Delivery accept with race-condition guard (only one partner per order)\n- Live GPS tracking (update + read)\n- OTP generation, verification, and auto-mark-delivered\n\n### Gaps / bugs\n- `GET /orders/` returns **all orders with no auth** (intended as admin, unprotected)\n- `assign-delivery` route ignores return value from `assign_delivery_partner()` and omits `partner_vehicle`\n- `VALID_STATUS` includes `\"Accepted\"` but model also references legacy `\"Placed\"` (not in route validation)\n- Debug `print()` statements left in order model and OTP verify route\n- Review submission tries `order_collection.update_one({\"_id\": data[\"order_id\"]})` using a **string** instead of `ObjectId` — likely **fails silently** to mark `review_submitted`\n\n---\n\n## 5. Restaurant / Menu System\n\n### Works\n- CRUD for restaurants (`/restaurants`) — `GET /` embeds each restaurant's menu\n- CRUD for menu items (`/menu`) keyed by `restaurant_email`\n- Restaurant owner register/login (separate `restaurant_owners` collection)\n- Owner dashboard (`GET /dashboard/{email}`) — order count, revenue from delivered orders, menu item count\n- Analytics: best-selling items (`GET /analytics/best-selling/{email}`)\n\n### Gaps\n- **No link** between `restaurant_owners` and `restaurants` collections — owner registration does not auto-create a restaurant record\n- Restaurant `rating` in schema is **client-supplied**, not computed from reviews API\n- Dashboard returns **hardcoded rating `4.8`**\n- Default GPS coordinates hardcoded to Pune (`18.52043, 73.856743`) in schemas\n- All restaurant/menu endpoints are **unauthenticated** — anyone can create/edit/delete\n\n---\n\n## 7. Reviews, Notifications, Payments\n\n### Reviews — **Implemented**\n| Endpoint | Status |\n|----------|--------|\n| POST `/reviews/` | Works — duplicate check per `order_id` |\n| GET `/reviews/restaurant/{email}` | Works |\n| GET `/reviews/restaurant/rating/{email}` | Works — MongoDB aggregation |\n| GET `/reviews/delivery/rating/{phone}` | Works — MongoDB aggregation |\n\nBug: marking `review_submitted` on the order document likely broken (ObjectId mismatch).\n\n### Notifications — **Not implemented (backend)**\n- `notification_collection` declared in `database.py`, zero routes\n- Frontend has `OrderNotification` and `LiveDeliveryNotification` components (browser audio/UI only)\n\n### Payments — **Not implemented**\n- `payment_method` is a **string field** on orders (`\"Cash on Delivery\"` or `\"Online Payment\"`)\n- No Stripe/Razorpay/UPI gateway, no payment verification, no webhooks\n- \"Online Payment\" in frontend is UI-only (`PaymentMethods.tsx`) — no backend processing\n\n---\n\n## 8. Mock Data and Incomplete Implementations\n\n| Item | Location | Notes |\n|------|----------|-------|\n| Hardcoded JWT secret | `auth/security.py` | `\"CHANGE_THIS_TO_A_LONG_RANDOM_SECRET_KEY_FOR_PRODUCTION\"` |\n| Hardcoded ratings | `models/dashboard.py` (4.8), `routes/delivery_dashboard.py` (4.9) | Not from `reviews` collection |\n| Hardcoded earnings | `delivery_dashboard.py` | Flat ₹50 per delivery |\n| Default GPS | `schemas/order.py`, `schemas/restaurant.py` | Pune coordinates |\n| Empty admin router | `routers/admin.py` | Not mounted |\n| Empty auth module | `auth/auth.py`, `auth/__init__.py` | Stubs |\n| Unused DB collections | `carts`, `wishlist`, `coupons`, `notifications` | No implementation |\n| Utility scripts | `reset_delivery_password.py`, `check_delivery.py` | Dev helpers with example email `rahul.sharma@example.com` |\n| Debug logging | `models/order.py`, `routes/order.py` | `print()` for OTP/status |\n\nNo formal seed-data scripts found in the backend.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Environment Variables\n\n### Backend (`E:/campusbite/campusbite/backend/.env`)\n| Variable | Used in | Purpose |\n|----------|---------|---------|\n| `MONGODB_URL` | `db/database.py` | MongoDB Atlas connection string |\n| `DATABASE_NAME` | `db/database.py` | Database name (`CampusBite`) |\n\n### Not env-driven (hardcoded)\n| Item | Location |\n|------|----------|\n| `SECRET_KEY` | `auth/security.py` |\n| CORS origins | `main.py` (`localhost:3000` only) |\n\n### Frontend (related, not backend)\n| Variable | File |\n|----------|------|\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` | Various `src/services/*.ts` (defaults to `http://127.0.0.1:8000`) |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` | `.env.local` |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Security Concerns\n\n### Critical\n1. **No API authentication** — JWTs are issued but never validated; all endpoints are publicly callable\n2. **Hardcoded JWT secret** in source code\n3. **OTP exposed without auth** — `GET /orders/otp/{order_id}` returns delivery OTP to anyone with an order ID\n4. **MongoDB credentials in `.env`** — contains live Atlas username/password (credential exposure risk if committed or shared)\n5. **Google Maps API key** in `.env.local` (frontend)\n\n### High\n6. **Unprotected admin endpoints** — `GET /orders/`, `GET /test-db` expose all data\n7. **Unprotected write endpoints** — anyone can create/delete restaurants, menu items, change order status\n8. **Customer PII in URLs** — orders fetched by phone number with no ownership check (`/orders/customer/{phone}`)\n9. **Delivery partner impersonation** — accept orders, update location, verify OTP with only `order_id` + body data\n\n### Medium\n10. **CORS limited to localhost** — production deployment will need updating\n11. **No rate limiting** — brute-force login, OTP guessing\n12. **Inconsistent input validation** — restaurant owner and delivery login use raw `dict` instead of Pydantic schemas\n13. **Debug prints leak OTP values** to server logs\n14. **`update_status` upsert** — arbitrary phone can create partner records\n15. **No HTTPS enforcement** — dev HTTP only\n\n### Low\n16. **24-hour token expiry** with no refresh/revocation\n17. **No password strength rules** beyond Pydantic `EmailStr` on some schemas\n\n---\n\n## Summary: What Works vs Stubs\n\n| Feature | Status |\n|---------|--------|\n| Customer auth (register/login) | **Works** — tokens issued, not enforced |\n| Restaurant owner auth | **Works** — same |\n| Delivery partner auth | **Works** — includes `role` claim |\n| Restaurants CRUD | **Works** — no auth |\n| Menu CRUD | **Works** — no auth |\n| Order placement & lifecycle | **Works** — full status machine |\n| Delivery accept/track/OTP | **Works** — no auth |\n| Reviews & ratings API | **Works** — order flag update likely broken |\n| Dashboard / analytics | **Works** — hardcoded ratings |\n| Cart / wishlist | **Frontend only** |\n| Coupons | **Stub** (collection only) |\n| Notifications | **Stub** (collection only) |\n| Payments | **Stub** (string field only) |\n| Admin API | **Empty stub** |\n| JWT middleware / RBAC | **Not implemented** |\n\nThe backend is a functional prototype with real MongoDB persistence for core food-ordering flows, but it is **not production-ready** from a security standpoint — treat it as an open API until authentication middleware and authorization are added to every sensitive route.\n\n[REDACTED]"}]}}

Hardcoded `127.0.0.1:8000` URLs\n\nAll API traffic defaults to `http://127.0.0.1:8000` when `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` is unset.\n\n**Always hardcoded (ignore env):**\n- `src/services/adminService.ts` (entire file)\n- `src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx`\n- `src/components/restaurant/RestaurantLoginForm.tsx`\n- `src/components/delivery/DeliveryLoginForm.tsx`\n- `src/app/restaurant/dashboard/page.tsx`\n- `src/app/restaurant/dashboard/menu/add/page.tsx`\n- `src/app/restaurant/dashboard/menu/edit/[id]/page.tsx`\n- `src/app/delivery/dashboard/page.tsx` (stats call)\n- `src/app/delivery/dashboard/history/page.tsx`\n\n**Use env with fallback:** `orderService`, `deliveryService`, `deliveryPartnerService`, and most other pages.\n\n**No `.env` file** exists in the repo.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Auth Flow (JWT, Storage, Role Routing)\n\n### Customer (`AuthContext`)\n- **Login:** `POST /auth/login` → stores `token` + `user` `{name, email}` in localStorage\n- **Guard:** Only `/profile` redirects to `/login` if not logged in\n- **JWT usage:** Token is **never sent** on API requests; orders are looked up by **phone from checkout localStorage**\n- **Register:** Empty page — no API\n- **Role routing:** None — all customers go to `/` after login\n\n### Restaurant owner (separate auth)\n- **Login:** `POST /restaurant-owner/login` → `restaurantToken` (JWT) + `restaurantOwner` `{ownerName, restaurantName}` — **email NOT saved**\n- **Guard:** `restaurant/dashboard/layout.tsx` checks `restaurantToken` exists only (no server validation)\n- **JWT usage:** Token **never sent** on menu/order/dashboard API calls\n- **Bug:** Menu/orders use `owner.email || \"owner@test.com\"` — login never stores `email`, so APIs likely hit wrong account\n\n### Delivery partner (separate auth)\n- **Login:** `POST /delivery/login` → `deliveryPartner` object in localStorage (no JWT)\n- **Guard:** **None** on dashboard layout — pages degrade if partner missing\n- **Logout:** Only on profile page; sidebar logout button is **non-functional**\n\n### Admin\n- **No auth at all**\n\n### Global\n- No `middleware.ts`\n- No role-based route protection\n- Four independent auth silos with different localStorage keys\n\n---\n\n## 7. Cart, Checkout, Order Placement, Tracking\n\n### Cart (`CartContext`)\n- Items in React state + `localStorage` key `\"cart\"`\n- Fields: `id`, `name`, `price`, `image`, `quantity` — **`restaurant_email` in type but MenuCard never sets it**\n- Purely client-side; no server sync\n\n### Checkout (`CheckoutContext`)\n- Address, phone, payment, GPS, restaurant coords in `localStorage` key `\"checkout\"`\n- `RestaurantCheckoutSetup` sets `restaurant_email` + lat/lng when visiting a restaurant page\n- `AddressForm` captures GPS via `navigator.geolocation` for \"self\" orders\n- `PaymentMethods`: COD or \"Online Payment\" — **UI only, no payment gateway**\n\n### Order placement (`OrderSummary` → `placeOrder`)\n- Validates cart + checkout fields client-side\n- `POST /orders/` with items, address, GPS, restaurant coords, `delivery_for`, hardcoded ₹40 delivery fee\n- Clears cart → redirects to `/order-success?orderId=...` (success page **ignores** `orderId` query param)\n\n### Order tracking\n- **`/orders/[id]`:** Polls `GET /orders/{id}` every 5s; status timeline, partner info, Google Maps link\n- **`/my-orders`:** Polls by phone; shows OTP, live track button, review modal\n- **`/track-order/[id]`:** Polls `GET /orders/delivery/location/{id}`; renders `LiveTrackingMap` (Google Maps Directions API)\n\n**Gap:** Customer login/JWT not tied to orders — identity is checkout phone number only.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Restaurant Owner Dashboard & Menu Management\n\n### Dashboard (`/restaurant/dashboard`)\n- **Real API:** `GET /dashboard/{email}`, `GET /analytics/best-selling/{email}`\n- Shows orders, revenue, menu count, rating, best-selling chart\n- 5s auto-refresh\n- **Risk:** `email` fallback `\"owner@test.com\"` because login doesn't persist email\n\n### Menu management\n- **List:** `GET /menu/{email}` — toggle availability, delete\n- **Add:** `POST /menu/` — hardcoded URL\n- **Edit:** `GET /menu/item/{id}`, `PUT /menu/{id}` — hardcoded URL\n- Full CRUD against backend when API is running\n\n### Orders (`/restaurant/dashboard/orders`)\n- **Real API:** `GET /orders/restaurant/{email}`, status updates via `PUT /orders/{id}/{status}`\n- Workflow: Pending → Accepted → Preparing → Ready for Pickup; Cancel/Reject\n- 5s polling\n\n### Missing sidebar targets\nAnalytics, Restaurant profile, Settings — **no pages exist**.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Customer\n\nThe customer can:\n\nRegister/login\nBrowse restaurants\nBrowse menus\nAdd food to cart\nSelect quantity\nEnter delivery address\nUse GPS location\nOrder for themselves\nOrder for someone else\nSelect payment method\nPlace order\nView orders\nTrack orders\nReceive delivery\nVerify delivery using OTP\n\nThis is the part we've already built quite far.\n\n5. 🍴 Restaurant\n\nA restaurant/vendor can:\n\nRegister\nCreate restaurant profile\nAdd restaurant information\nAdd menu items\nSet prices\nSet availability\nReceive orders\nAccept/reject orders\nPrepare orders\nMark order as ready\nHand order to delivery partner\n\nYour restaurant system supports different kinds of vendors conceptually:\n\nRestaurant\nMess\nHome Cook\nTiffin Provider\nLocal Food Shop\n\nThis is one of the important differences between CampusBite and a conventional restaurant-only platform.\n\n6. 🚴 Delivery Partner\n\nThis is the part we were just starting.\n\nA delivery partner can:\n\nLogin\n  ↓\nSee available orders\n  ↓\nAccept order\n  ↓\nGo to restaurant\n  ↓\nPick up food\n  ↓\nStart delivery\n  ↓\nShare GPS\n  ↓\nReach customer\n  ↓\nCustomer provides OTP\n  ↓\nOrder Delivered\n\nThe delivery partner dashboard we just started building is therefore not a random extra page.\n\nIt completes the actual food-delivery cycle.\n\n7. 🛠️ Admin\n\nThe administrator is the person controlling the CampusBite platform.\n\nAdmin can eventually manage:\n\nUsers\nRestaurants\nMesses\nMenu items\nOrders\nDelivery partners\nComplaints\nPayments\nSubscriptions\nReviews\nPlatform settings\n\nThink of Admin as the control center.\n\n8. The Most Important CampusBite Flow\n\nLet's take one real example.\n\nSuppose you order:\n\nVed Pizza — ₹200\n\nStep 1 — Customer\n\nYou open:\n\nRestaurants\n   ↓\nPizza Palace\n   ↓\nVed Pizza\n   ↓\nAdd to Cart\nStep 2 — Checkout\n\nYou enter:\n\nName\nMobile\nAddress\nCity\nPIN\nLandmark\nGPS\nPayment method\n\nYou can choose:\n\nMyself\n\nor\n\nSomeone else\n\nThis is important because you might order food for:\n\nYour friend, family member, roommate, etc.\n\nat another location.\n\n9. GPS System\n\nCampusBite stores two important locations.\n\nCustomer location\ncustomer_latitude\ncustomer_longitude\n\nThis tells the delivery partner where the food needs to go.\n\nRestaurant location\nrestaurant_latitude\nrestaurant_longitude\n\nThis tells the delivery partner where the food needs to be picked up.\n\nEventually:\n\nRestaurant\n   📍\n   │\n   │\n   │ Delivery Partner\n   ↓\nCustomer\n   📍\n\nThis is the foundation for live delivery tracking.\n\n10. Order Lifecycle\n\nThis is one of the most important concepts in your entire project.\n\nAn order shouldn't simply be:\n\nOrder → Delivered\n\nIt needs states.\n\nFor example:\n\nPending\n   ↓\nAccepted\n   ↓\nPreparing\n   ↓\nReady for Pickup\n   ↓\nAssigned\n   ↓\nPicked Up\n   ↓\nOut for Delivery\n   ↓\nDelivered\n\nAnd potentially:\n\nPending\n   ↓\nCancelled\n\nThis order-status system connects Customer + Restaurant + Delivery Partner.\n\n11. Example of the Complete System\n\nImagine you order a ₹200 pizza.\n\nCustomer\n\nPlaces order.\n\nOrder #ABC123\n₹240 Total\nStatus: Pending\nRestaurant\n\nReceives it.\n\nNew Order\n     ↓\nAccept\n     ↓\nPreparing\n     ↓\nReady for Pickup\nDelivery Partner\n\nSees:\n\nAvailable Order\n₹240\nPizza Palace → Customer Address\n\n[Accept Order]\n\nAccepts it.\n\nAssigned\n\nPicks it up.\n\nPicked Up\n\nStarts delivery.\n\nOut for Delivery\n📍 GPS Active\n\nCustomer opens My Orders.\n\nOut for Delivery\n      ↓\nDelivery Partner approaching\n      ↓\nDelivered\n\nCustomer gives OTP.\n\nOTP: 4821\n      ↓\nVerified\n      ↓\nDelivered ✅\n\nThat's the actual CampusBite ecosystem we're building.\n\n12. Mess Subscription — Your Special Feature\n\nThis is potentially one of CampusBite's strongest differentiators.\n\nInstead of:\n\nOrder lunch every day\nOrder dinner every day\n\nA student can subscribe.\n\nFor example:\n\nCampusBite Mess Subscription\n\n₹3,000 / Month\n\nLunch\n+\nDinner\n+\nRegular menu\n+\nStudent pricing\n\nThe exact pricing will depend on the mess.\n\nThe important concept is:\n\nCampusBite isn't only an on-demand food delivery platform; it can also provide recurring student food plans.\n\n13. Student Specials\n\nYour original concept also included budget-focused food categories.\n\nFor example:\n\nStudent Specials\n\nUnder ₹50\nUnder ₹80\nUnder ₹100\nUnder ₹150\n\nThis makes the platform specifically useful for students.\n\nInstead of browsing hundreds of expensive restaurant items, a student can quickly find:\n\n\"What can I eat for ₹80?\"\n\n14. Restaurant Discovery\n\nThe customer should eventually be able to search/filter by:\n\nBudget\n₹0–₹50\n₹50–₹100\n₹100–₹150\n₹150+\nFood type\nVeg\nNon-Veg\nCuisine\nPizza\nIndian\nChinese\nFast Food\netc.\nDistance\nNearby\n1 km\n3 km\n5 km\nRating\n4+\n4.5+\n\nThis makes CampusBite much more useful than simply showing a restaurant list.\n\n15.
